Content: The Cell and Capacity Assistance Information E-UTRA IE is used by the NG-RAN node to request information about E-UTRA cells and it includes information about cell list size capacity.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Cell Assistance Information E-UTRA
Content: The Cell Assistance Information IE is used by the NG-RAN node to request information about E-UTRA cells.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Maximum Cell List Size
Content: This IE indicates the maximum size the sending node can handle for a given cell list.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Message Oversize Notification
Content: This IE indicates that a failure has occurred due to an excessive message size and it indicates the maximum number of cells that can be received in the List of Served Cells NR IE or in the List of Served Cells E-UTRA IE.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Partial List Indicator
Content: The Partial List Indicator IE is used by the NG-RAN node to indicate whether the served cell information contained in the same message is a partial list.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Offset of NB-IoT Channel Number to EARFCN
Content: This IE is used to indicate the offset of the NB-IoT Channel Number to the EARFCN (TS 36.104 ).
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > NB-IoT UL DL Alignment Offset
Content: This IE is used to indicate the offset between the UL carrier frequency center with respect to DL carrier frequency center.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > TNL Capacity Indicator
Content: The TNL Capacity Indicator IE indicates the offered and available capacity of the Transport Network experienced by the NG RAN cell
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Radio Resource Status
Content: The Radio Resource Status IE indicates the usage of the PRBs per cell for MIMO, per SSB area , and per slice for all traffic in Downlink and Uplink and the usage of PDCCH CCEs for Downlink and Uplink scheduling. 9.2.2. 51 Composite Available Capacity Group The Composite Available Capacity Group IE indicates the overall available resource level per cell and per SSB area in the cell in Downlink, Uplink and Supplementary Uplink. 9.2.2. 52 Composite Available Capacity The Composite Available Capacity IE indicates the overall available resource level in the cell in either Downlink or Uplink.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Cell Capacity Class Value
Content: The Cell Capacity Class Value IE indicates the value that classifies the cell capacity with regards to the other cells. The Cell Capacity Class Value IE only indicates resources that are configured for traffic purposes.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Capacity Value
Content: The Capacity Value IE indicates the amount of resources per cell and per SSB area that are available relative to the total NG-RAN resources. The capacity value should be measured and reported so that the minimum NG-RAN resource usage of existing services is reserved according to implementation. The Capacity Value IE can be weighted according to the ratio of cell capacity class values, if available.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Slice Available Capacity
Content: The Slice Available Capacity IE indicates the amount of resources per network slice that are available per cell relative to the total NG-RAN resources per cell. The Slice Available Capacity Value Downlink IE and the Slice Available Capacity Value Uplink IE can be weighted according to the ratio of the corresponding cell capacity class values contained in the Composite Available Capacity Group IE , if available.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > RRC Connections
Content: The RRC Connections IE indicates the overall status of RRC connections per cell.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Number of RRC Connections
Content: The Number of RRC Connections IE indicates the maximum supported number of UEs in RRC_CONNECTED mode.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Available RRC Connection Capacity Value
Content: The Available RRC Connection Capacity Value IE indicates the residual percentage of the number of RRC connections, relative to the maximum number of RRC connections supported by the cell.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > UE RLF Report
Content: This IE contains the RLF Report to be transferred.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Mobility Parameters Information
Content: The Mobility Parameters Information IE contains the change of the Handover Trigger as compared to its current value. The Handover Trigger corresponds to the threshold at which a cell initialises the handover preparation procedure towards a specific neighbour cell. Positive value of the change means the handover is proposed to take place later.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Mobility Parameters Modification Range
Content: The Mobility Parameters Modification Range IE contains the range of Handover Trigger Change values permitted by the NG-RAN node at the moment the MOBILITY CHANGE FAILURE message is sent.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Number of Active UEs
Content: The Number of Active UEs IE indicates the mean number of active UEs as defined in TS 38.314 .
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > NR Carrier List
Content: This IE indicates the SCS-specific carriers per TDD, per DL, per UL or per SUL of an NR cell. 9.2.2. 64 SSB Positions In Burst Indicates the time domain positions of the transmitted SS-blocks in a half frame with SS/PBCH blocks as defined in TS 38.213 , clause 4.1.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > NID
Content: This IE is used to identify (together with a PLMN identifier) a Standalone Non-Public Network. The NID is specified in TS 23.003 .
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > CAG-Identifier
Content: This IE is used to identify (together with a PLMN identifier) a Public Network Integrated Non-Public Network. The CAG-Identifier is specified in TS 23.003 .
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Broadcast CAG-Identifier List
Content: This IE contains a list of CAG-Identifiers.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > NPN Broadcast Information
Content: This IE contains NPN related broadcast information.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > NPN Support
Content: This IE contains NPN related information associated with Network Slicing information.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Global Cell Identity
Content: This IE is used to globally identify an NG-RAN cell or an E-UTRAN cell (see TS 36.300 ).
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > NPRACH Configuration
Content: This IE indicates the NPRACH Configuration.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > SFN Offset
Content: This IE contains the time offset between an absolute time reference and the SFN0 start. The IE is calculated assuming that the SFN transmission started at the absolute time reference. The absolute time reference chosen is 1980-01-06 T00:00:19 International Atomic Time (TAI).
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > CHO Configuration
Content: This IE contains the CHO configuration information.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > SSB Offset Information
Content: This IE represents the SSB Offset to apply to UE measurements received for the SSB Area identified by the SSB Index.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > SSB Offset Modification Range
Content: The SSB Offset Modification Range IE contains the range of SSB Offset values permitted by the NG-RAN node at the moment the MOBILITY CHANGE FAILURE message is sent.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Multiplexing Info
Content: This IE contains information about the multiplexing capabilities between the IAB-DU’s cell and the cells configured on the co-located IAB-MT.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Traffic Index
Content: This IE is used to identify the traffic offloaded to the topology of non-F1-terminating IAB-donor. This IE is only applicable to IAB.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Traffic Profile
Content: This IE indicates the QoS parameters for F1-U traffic, or the non-UP traffic type. This IE is only applicable to IAB.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > F1-Terminating Topology BH Information
Content: This IE provides the BH information of the traffic used in F1-terminating IAB- donor’s topology. This IE is only applicable to IAB.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Non-F1-terminating Topology BH Information
Content: This IE provides the BH information of the traffic used in non-F1-terminating IAB- donor’s topology. This IE is only applicable to IAB.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Traffic To Be Released Information
Content: This IE is used to indicate the offloaded traffic to be released . This IE is only applicable to IAB.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > IAB TNL Address Request
Content: This IE indicates the request of IP address assignment, and/or the request of IP address removal. This IE is only applicable to IAB.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > IAB TNL Address Response
Content: This IE indicates the TNL address(es) assigned to IAB node(s).
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > BAP Routing ID
Content: This IE indicates the BAP Routing ID. This IE is only applicable to IAB.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > BH RLC Channel ID
Content: This IE uniquely identifies a BH RLC channel in the link between IAB-MT of the IAB-node and IAB-DU of the parent IAB-node or IAB-donor-DU.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > BAP Address
Content: This IE indicates the BAP address of an IAB-node or of an IAB-donor-DU, and it is part of the BAP Routing ID.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > BAP Path ID
Content: This IE indicates the BAP Path ID, which is part of the BAP Routing ID. This IE is only applicable to IAB.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > IAB QoS mapping information
Content: This IE indicates the DSCP and/or IPv6 Flow Label field(s) of an IP packet of the traffic offloaded to the non-F1-terminating IAB- donor topology. This IE is only used for IAB.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > IAB TNL Address
Content: This IE indicates an IPv4 or IPv6 address or an IPv6 address prefix assigned to an IAB-node.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > IAB TNL Addresses Requested
Content: This IE indicates the number of IPv4 or IPv6 addresses or IPv6 address prefixes requested for the indicated usage.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > IAB Cell Information
Content: This IE contains IAB-DU cell resource configuration, cell specific signal/channel configuration and multiplexing info of the cell of an IAB-node or IAB-donor-DU.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > gNB-DU Cell Resource Configuration
Content: This IE contains the resource configuration of the cells served by a gNB-DU, i.e. the TDD/FDD resource parameters for each activated cell (TS 38.213 , clause 11.1.1). This IE is only applicable if the gNB-DU is an IAB-DU or an IAB-donor-DU.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > IAB STC Info
Content: This IE contains cell SSB Transmission Configuration (STC) information of an IAB-DU or an IAB-donor-DU.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > RB Set Configuration
Content: This IE contains the configuration for up to M non-overlapping RB sets for a given gNB-DU cell, used for frequency domain resource allocation. This IE is only applicable if the gNB-DU is an IAB-DU. The maximum value of M is 8.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > IAB TNL Address Exception
Content: This IE indicates the list of source TNL addresses carried on UL IP packets in an IAB network, which can be forwarded over the inter-IAB- donor- DU tunnel, and that are exempt from TNL address filtering, for the purpose of inter-donor-DU rerouting.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > BH Info List
Content: This IE indicates a list of BH information ind ices , where each index represents the offloaded traffic pertaining to, e.g., a certain BAP routing ID, BH RLC CH.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Non-UP traffic
Content: This IE indicates the type of non-UP traffic.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Local NG-RAN Node Identifier
Content: This IE is used to resolve a Global NG-RAN Node ID from an I-RNTI and obtain a reference to an UE context at RRC Resume.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Served Cell Specific Info Request
Content: The Served Cell Specific Info Request IE is used by the NG-RAN node to request specific information about NR cells.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > CPAC Configuration
Content: This IE contains the CPC or CPA configuration information.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Radio Resource Status NR-U
Content: The Radio Resource Status NR-U IE indicates the usage of the PRBs per NR-U Channel for all traffic in Downlink and Uplink.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Mobile IAB Authorization Status
Content: This IE indicates the authorization status of the mobile IAB node.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > NG-RAN Node and Cell Configuration related IE definitions > Mobile IAB Cell
Content: This IE indicates that the cell is served by a mobile IAB-node.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Message Type
Content: The Message Type IE uniquely identifies the message being sent. It is mandatory for all messages.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Cause
Content: The purpose of the Cause IE is to indicate the reason for a particular event for the XnAP protocol. The meaning of the different cause values is specified in the following table. In general, "not supported" cause values indicate that the related capability is missing. On the other hand, "not available" cause values indicate that the related capability is present, but insufficient resources were available to perform the requested action.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Criticality Diagnostics
Content: The Criticality Diagnostics IE is sent by the NG-RAN node when parts of a received message have not been comprehended or were missing, or if the message contained logical errors. When applicable, it contains information about which IEs were not comprehended or were missing.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Bit Rate
Content: This IE indicates the number of bits delivered by NG-RAN in UL or to NG-RAN in DL or by the UE in sidelink within a period of time, divided by the duration of the period. It is used, for example, to indicate the maximum or guaranteed bit rate for a GBR QoS flow, or an aggregate maximum bit rate.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > QoS Flow Level QoS Parameters
Content: This IE defines the QoS Parameters to be applied to a QoS flow.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > GBR QoS Flow Information
Content: This IE indicates QoS Parameters for a GBR QoS Flow for downlink and uplink.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Allocation and Retention Priority
Content: This IE specifies the relative importance compared to other QoS flows for allocation and retention of the NR RAN resource.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Non dynamic 5QI Descriptor
Content: This IE defines QoS characteristics for a standardized or pre-configured 5QI for downlink and uplink.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Dynamic 5QI Descriptor
Content: This IE defines the QoS characteristics for a non-standardized or not pre-configured 5QI for downlink and uplink.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > QoS Flow Identifier
Content: This IE identifies either a QoS Flow within a PDU Session or an MBS QoS flow within an MBS Session. Definition and use of the QoS Flow Identifier is specified in TS 23.501 .
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Packet Loss Rate
Content: This IE indicates the Packet Loss Rate for a QoS flow.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Packet Delay Budget
Content: This IE indicates the Packet Delay Budget for a QoS flow.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Packet Error Rate
Content: This IE indicates the Packet Error Rate for a QoS flow.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Averaging Window
Content: This IE indicates the Averaging Window for a QoS flow and applies to GBR QoS flows only.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Maximum Data Burst Volume
Content: This IE indicates the Maximum Data Burst Volume for a QoS flow and applies to delay critical GBR QoS flows only.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > NG-RAN node UE XnAP ID
Content: The NG-RAN node UE XnAP ID uniquely identifies a UE over the Xn interface within the NG-RAN node. The use of this IE is defined in TS 38.401 . NOTE: If Xn-C signalling transport is shared among multiple interface instances, the value of the NG-RAN node UE XnAP ID is allocated so that it can be associated with the corresponding Xn-C interface instance.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > UE Aggregate Maximum Bit Rate
Content: The UE Aggregate Maximum Bitrate is applicable for all Non-GBR QoS flows per UE which is defined for the Downlink and the Uplink direction and a subscription parameter provided by the AMF to the NG-RAN.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > PDU Session ID
Content: This IE identifies a PDU Session for a UE. Definition and use of the PDU Session ID is specified in TS 23.501 .
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > PDU Session Type
Content: This IE defines the PDU Session Type as specified in TS 23.501 .
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > TAI Support List
Content: This IE indicates the list of TAIs supported by NG-RAN node and associated characteristics e.g. supported slices.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > S-NSSAI
Content: This IE indicates the S-NSSAI as defined in TS 23.003 .
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Slice Support List
Content: This IE indicates the list of supported slices.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Index to RAT/Frequency Selection Priority
Content: The Index to RAT/Frequency Selection Priority IE is used to define local configuration for RRM strategies such as camp priorities and control of inter-RAT/inter-frequency mobility in RRC_CONNECTED, as specified in TS 23.501 .
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > GUAMI
Content: This IE contains the Globally Unique AMF Identifier (GUAMI) as defined in TS 23.003 .
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Target Cell Global ID
Content: This IE contains either an NR CGI or an E-UTRA CGI.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > AMF UE NGAP ID
Content: This IE is defined in TS 38.413 and used to uniquely identify the UE association over the source side NG interface instance.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > SCG Configuration Query
Content: The SCG Configuration Query IE is used to request the S-NG-RAN node to provide current SCG configuration.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > RLC Mode
Content: The RLC Mode IE indicates the RLC Mode used for a DRB.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Transport Layer Address
Content: This IE is defined to contain an IP address.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > UP Transport Layer Information
Content: This element is used to provide the transport layer information associated with NG or Xn user plane transport. In this release it corresponds to an IP adress and a GTP Tunnel Endpoint Identifier. When the NR-DC UE is connected with an IAB, the QoS Mapping Information is used to set the IP header of packets in case that the S-NG-RAN node serves the IAB and the packets belonging to MN-terminated split bearer/SCG bearer are transmitted from M-NG-RAN node to S-NG-RAN node, and in case that the M-NG-RAN node serves the IAB and the packets belonging to SN-terminated split bearer/MCG bearer are transmitted from S-NG-RAN node to M-NG-RAN node.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > CP Transport Layer Information
Content: This element is used to provide the transport layer information associated with NG or Xn control plane transport.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Masked IMEISV
Content: This information element contains the IMEISV value with a mask, to identify a terminal model without identifying an individual Mobile Equipment.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > DL Forwarding
Content: This element indicates a proposal for forwarding of downlink packets.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Data Forwarding Accepted
Content: This element indicates that data forwarding was accepted.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > COUNT Value for PDCP SN Length 12
Content: This information element indicates the 12-bit long PDCP sequence number and the corresponding 20 bits long Hyper Frame Number.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > COUNT Value for PDCP SN Length 18
Content: This information element indicates the 18-bit long PDCP sequence number and the corresponding 14 bits long Hyper Frame Number.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > RAN Paging Area
Content: The RAN Paging Area IE defines the paging area within a PLMN for RAN paging a UE in RRC_INACTIVE state.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > UE Context ID
Content: This IE is used to address a UE Context within an NG-RAN node.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Assistance Data for RAN Paging
Content: This IE provides assistance information for RAN paging.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > RAN Paging Attempt Information
Content: This IE includes information related to the RAN paging attempt over Xn.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > UE RAN Paging Identity
Content: The IE defines the UE Identity for RAN paging a UE in RRC_INACTIVE.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Paging Priority
Content: This information element contains an indication of the priority to be considered for the paging request.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Delivery Status
Content: This IE provides the delivery status of RRC PDUs provided by RRC Transfer message.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > I-RNTI
Content: The I-RNTI is defined for allocation in an NR or E-UTRA serving cell as a reference to a UE Context within an NG-RAN node. The I-RNTI is partitioned into two parts, the first part identifies the NG-RAN node that allocated the I-RNTI and the second part identifies the UE context stored in this NG-RAN node, refer to Annex C in TS 38.300 , or t he I-RNTI is partitioned into three parts, the first part indicates the length of NG-RAN Node ID part of the NG-RAN Node that allocated the I-RNTI, the second part identifies the NG-RAN node that allocated the I-RNTI and the third part identifies the UE context stored in this NG-RAN node , refer to Annex F in TS 38.300 .
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Location Reporting Information
Content: This information element indicates how the location information should be reported.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Area of Interest Information
Content: This IE contains indicates the Area of Interest information, which may contain multiple Areas of Interest, as specified in TS 23.502 .
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > UE Security Capabilities
Content: The UE Security Capabilities IE defines the supported algorithms for encryption and integrity protection in the UE. Except as noted below, the NG-RAN nodes store and send the complete bitmaps without modification or truncation as specified in TS 38.300 . NOTE: There is a 1-bit circular shift between the bitmaps of the IE in this specification and the corresponding bitmaps in TS 38.413 .
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > AS Security Information
Content: The AS Security Information IE is used to generate the key material to be used for AS security with the UE.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > S-NG-RAN node Security Key
Content: The S-NG-RAN node Security Key IE is used to apply security in the S-NG-RAN node as defined in TS 33.501 .
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Security Indication
Content: This IE contains the user plane integrity protection indication and confidentiality protection indication which indicates the requirements on UP integrity protection and ciphering for the corresponding PDU session, respectively. Additionally, this IE contains the maximum integrity protected data rate values (UL and DL) per UE for integrity protected DRBs.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Mobility Restriction List
Content: This IE defines roaming or access restrictions for subsequent mobility actions for which the NG-RAN provides information about the target of the mobility action towards the UE, e.g., handover, or for SCG selection during dual connectivity operation or for assigning proper RNAs. If the NG-RAN receives the Mobility Restriction List IE, it shall overwrite previously received restriction information. NG-RAN behaviour upon receiving this IE is specified in TS 23.501 .
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Xn Benefit Value
Content: The Xn Benefit Value IE indicates the quantified benefit of the signalling connection.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Trace Activation
Content: This IE defines parameters related to a trace activation.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Time To Wait
Content: This IE defines the minimum allowed waiting times.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > QoS Flow Notification Control Indication Info
Content: This IE provides information about QoS flows of a PDU Session Resource for which notification control has been requested.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Request Reporting Reference ID
Content: This IE contains the Request Reporting Reference ID and is used for UE presence in Area of Interest reporting as specified in TS 23.502 .
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > User plane traffic activity report
Content: This IE is used to indicate user plane traffic activity.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Lower Layer presence status change
Content: This IE is used to indicate that lower layer resources’ presence status shall be changed. If the presence status is set to "release lower layers" or "suspend lower layers", SDAP entities, PDCP entities, Xn-U bearer resources, NG-U bearer resources and UE context information shall be kept.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > RRC Resume Cause
Content: The purpose of the RRC Resume Cause IE is to indicate to the old NG-RAN node the reason for the RRC Connection Resume as received from the UE in the resumeCause-r15 defined in TS 36.331 or in the resumeCause defined in TS 38.331 . In this version of the specification, this is limited to the case of RNA update.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Priority Level
Content: This IE indicates the Priority Level for a QoS flow.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > PDCP SN Length
Content: The PDCP SN Length IE is used to indicate the PDCP SN length configuration of the bearer.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > UE History Information
Content: The UE History Information IE contains information about cells that a UE has been served by in active state prior to the target cell. The overall mechanism is described in TS 36.300 . NOTE: The definition of this IE is aligned with the definition of the UE History Information IE in TS 38.413 .
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Information Element definitions > General IE definitions > Last Visited Cell Information
Content: The Last Visited Cell Information may contain cell specific information.

Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Message and Information Element Abstract Syntax (with ASN.1) > General
Content: XnAP ASN.1 definition conforms to ITU-T Rec. X.680 and ITU-T Rec. X.681 . Sub clause 9.3 presents the Abstract Syntax of the XnAP protocol with ASN.1. In case there is contradiction between the ASN.1 definition in this sub clause and the tabular format in sub clause 9.1 and 9.2, the ASN.1 shall take precedence, except for the definition of conditions for the presence of conditional elements, in which the tabular format shall take precedence. The ASN.1 definition specifies the structure and content of XnAP messages. XnAP messages can contain any IEs specified in the object set definitions for that message without the order or number of occurrence being restricted by ASN.1. However, for this version of the standard, a sending entity shall construct an XnAP message according to the PDU definitions module and with the following additional rules: IEs shall be ordered (in an IE container) in the order they appear in object set definitions. Object set definitions specify how many times IEs may appear. An IE shall appear exactly once if the presence field in an object has value "mandatory". An IE may appear at most once if the presence field in an object has value "optional" or "conditional". If in a tabular format there is multiplicity specified for an IE (i.e. an IE list) then in the corresponding ASN.1 definition the list definition is separated into two parts. The first part defines an IE container list in which the list elements reside. The second part defines list elements. The IE container list appears as an IE of its own. For this version of the standard an IE container list may contain only one kind of list elements. NOTE: In the above, "IE" means an IE in the object set with an explicit ID. If one IE needs to appear more than once in one object set, then the different occurrences have different IE IDs. If an XnAP message that is not constructed as defined above is received, this shall be considered as Abstract Syntax Error, and the message shall be handled as defined for Abstract Syntax Error in clause 10.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Message and Information Element Abstract Syntax (with ASN.1) > Usage of Private Message Mechanism for Non-standard Use
Content: The private message mechanism for non-standard use may be used: for special operator (and/or vendor) specific features considered not to be part of the basic functionality, i.e. the functionality required for a complete and high-quality specification in order to guarantee multivendor inter-operability. by vendors for research purposes, e.g. to implement and evaluate new algorithms/features before such features are proposed for standardisation. The private message mechanism shall not be used for basic functionality. Such functionality shall be standardised.
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Message and Information Element Abstract Syntax (with ASN.1) > Elementary Procedure Definitions
Content: -- ASN1START -- ************************************************************** -- -- Elementary Procedure definitions -- -- ************************************************************** XnAP-PDU-Descriptions \ itu-t (0) identified-organization (4) etsi (0) mobileDomain (0) ngran-access (22) modules (3) xnap (2) version1 (1) xnap-PDU-Descriptions (0) \ DEFINITIONS AUTOMATIC TAGS ::= BEGIN -- ************************************************************** -- -- IE parameter types from other modules. -- -- ************************************************************** IMPORTS Criticality, ProcedureCode FROM XnAP-CommonDataTypes HandoverRequest, HandoverRequestAcknowledge, HandoverPreparationFailure, SNStatusTransfer, UEContextRelease, HandoverCancel, pairs -- -- ************************************************************** XNAP-PROTOCOL-IES-PAIR ::= CLASS \ \&id ProtocolIE-ID UNIQUE, \&firstCriticality Criticality, \&FirstValue, TYPE \&SecondValue PRESENCE \&presence \ -- ************************************************************** -- -- Class Definition for Protocol Extensions -- -- ************************************************************** XNAP-PROTOCOL-EXTENSION ::= CLASS \ \&id ProtocolIE-ID UNIQUE, \&criticality Criticality, \&Extension, \&presence Presence \ WITH SYNTAX \ ID \&id CRITICALITY \&criticality EXTENSION \&Extension PRESENCE \&presence \ -- ************************************************************** -- -- Class Definition for Private IEs -- -- ProtocolIE-Single-Container \ XNAP-PROTOCOL-IES : IEsSetParam\ ::= ProtocolIE-Field \ \ IEsSetParam\ \ ProtocolIE-Field \ XNAP-PROTOCOL-IES : IEsSetParam\ ::= SEQUENCE \ id XNAP-PROTOCOL-IES.\&id (\ @id\ ), secondCriticality XNAP-PROTOCOL-IES-PAIR.\&secondCriticality (\ IEsSetParam\ \ @id\ ), secondValue XNAP-PROTOCOL-IES-PAIR.\&SecondValue (\ IEsSetParam\ \ @id\ ) \ -- XNAP-PRIVATE-IES.\&Value (\ IEsSetParam\ \ @id\ ) \ END -- ASN1STOP
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Message transfer syntax
Content: XnAP shall use the ASN.1 Basic Packed Encoding Rules (BASIC-PER) Aligned Variant as transfer syntax, as specified in ITU-T Rec. X.691 .
Title Path: Elements for XnAP Communication > Timers
Content: TXn - Specifies the maximum time for the Handover Preparation procedure in the source NG-RAN node. TXn - Specifies the maximum time for the protection of the overall handover procedure in the source NG-RAN node. TXn - Specifies the maximum time for the S-NG-RAN node Addition Preparation or M-NG-RAN node initiated S-NG-RAN node Modification Preparation. TXn - Specifies the maximum time in the S-NG-RAN node for either the S-NG-RAN node initiated S-NG-RAN node Modification procedure or the protection of the NG-RAN actions necessary to configure UE resources at S-NG-RAN node Addition or M-NG-RAN node initiated S-NG-RAN node Modification.
Title Path: Handling of unknown, unforeseen and erroneous protocol data
Content: Section 10 of TS 38.413 is applicable for the purposes of the present document.
